	div.setAttribute('id', 'cookie-policy');
	div.innerHTML = '<p class="mb-0"><img class="d-none d-md-inline mr-2" src="/res/assets/img/cookie.svg" alt="cookie policy"> PayProp uses cookies to remember you and deliver a personalised, responsive service. By continuing to use this site, you agree to our <a href="/terms">Terms of use</a> and <a href="/privacy">Privacy policy</a>. <a class="close-cookie-banner btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm d-block d-md-inline-block my-3 my-md-0 ml-md-2" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="removeMe()"><span>OK</span></a></p>';
	bodyTag.insertBefore(div, bodyTag.firstChild); // Adds the cookie banner after the opening <body>
	document.getElementsByTagName('body')[0].className += 'cookie-consent-visible'; // Adds a class to the <body> when the banner is visible

	createCookie(window.cookieName, window.cookieValue, window.cookieDuration); // Create the cookie
}

function createCookie(name, value, days) {
	if (days) {
		var date = new Date();
		date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
		var expires = "; expires=" + date.toGMTString();
	} else var expires = "";
	if (window.dropCookie) {
		document.cookie = name + "=" + value + expires + "; path=/";
	}
}

function checkCookie(name) {
	var nameEQ = name + "=";
	var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
	for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
		var c = ca[i];
		while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
		if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
	}
	return null;
}

function eraseCookie(name) {
	createCookie(name, "", -1);
}

window.removeMe = function removeMe() {
	var element = document.getElementById('cookie-policy');
	element.parentNode.removeChild(element);
};

if (checkCookie(window.cookieName) !== window.cookieValue) {
	createDiv();
}
</script>

<script>
  $(function () {

   // scroll functions, adds shadow and back to top button

   var header = $(".header, .subnav");
   var offset_nav = 10;
   var offset = 800;
   var duration = 300;

   // check if element is above the fold of the page (top or bottom). Returns boolean: true if the element is partially/fully on screen.
   $.fn.isVisible = function (checkTopOrBottom) {
      if (window.scrollY === undefined) {
         var screen_top_position = window.pageYOffset; // don't add 78px here because this is used by IE, which doesn't support sticky headers
      } else {
         var screen_top_position = window.scrollY + 78; // add 78px by default as new PP header is sticky everywhere
      }
      var screen_bottom_position = screen_top_position + window.innerHeight;

      var element_top_position = this.offset().top;
      var element_top_padding = this[0].style.paddingTop;
      var element_bottom_position = this.offset().top + this[0].offsetHeight;

      if (checkTopOrBottom == 'bottom') {
         return (screen_bottom_position >= (element_top_position + element_top_padding));
      } else if (checkTopOrBottom == 'top') {
         return (screen_top_position >= element_bottom_position);
      }
   }

   $(window).scroll(function () {
      if ($(this).scrollTop() > offset_nav) {
         header.removeClass('noShadows').addClass('justShadows');
      } else {
         header.removeClass('justShadows').addClass('noShadows');
      }

      if ($(this).scrollTop() > offset) {
         $('.back-to-top').fadeIn(duration);
      } else {
         $('.back-to-top').fadeOut(duration);
      }
   });

   $('.back-to-top').click(function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      $('html, body').animate({
         scrollTop: 0
      }, duration);
      return false;
   })

   // Match help options display message
   $('#help-options').change(function () {
      if ($(this).find(':selected').val() === 'opt-client') {
         $('.contact-form__message').show();
         $('.contact-form__message').text('For support, log in to your account or e-mail support@payprop.co.uk');
      } else if ($(this).find(':selected').val() === 'opt-tenant' || $(this).find(':selected').val() === 'opt-landlord') {
         $('.contact-form__message').show();
         $('.contact-form__message').text('For a faster response, please contact your letting agent.');
      } else {
         $('.contact-form__message').hide();
         $('.contact-form__message').text('');
      }
   });

   $('.input-submit').each(function (index) {
      $(this).click(function (event) {

         // identify id of form (in case multiple contact forms on one page)
         var formId = "#" + $(this).closest("form").attr("id");

         // disable submit button and hide errors
         $(this).addClass('disabled');
         $(formId + ' #input-error').hide();
         $(formId + ' #input-success').hide();

         var successMsg = '';
         var errorMsg = '';

         var input = $("<input>")
            .attr("type", "hidden")
            .attr("name", "url").val([window.location.protocol, '//', window.location.host, '//'].join(''));
         $(this).closest('form').append($(input));
         var source_input = $("<input>")
            .attr("type", "hidden")
            .attr("name", "source").val(getCookie('source'));
         $(this).closest('form').append($(source_input));

         $.post('/join', $(this).closest('form').serialize())
            .done(function (data) {
               if (data.success != undefined) {
                  if (data.success == 1) {
                     var ga_type = 'default';
                     var type = $('input[name=form]').val();
                     var propertyNum = $('#prop-num').val();
                     var helpType = $('#help-options').val();
                     switch (type) {
                        case 'index':
                           type = 'Homepage'
                           break;
                        case 'tour':
                           type = 'Features & Pricing'
                           break;
                        case 'contact':
                           type = 'Contact us'
                           ga_type = 'contactForm'
                           break;
                        case 'info':
                           type = 'Join PayProp'
                           ga_type = 'joinForm'
                           break;
                        case 'discover':
                           type = 'Discover PayProp'
                           break;
                        case 'hunters_top':
                           type = 'Hunters (Top)'
                           break;
                        case 'hunters_bottom':
                           type = 'Hunters (Bottom)'
                           break;
                        case 'hoorah':
                           type = 'Hoorah'
                           break;
                        case 'five_reasons_top':
                           type = 'Five Reasons (Top)'
                           break;
                        case 'five_reasons_bottom':
                           type = 'Five Reasons (Bottom)'
                           break;
                        default:
                           type = 'Unknown form!'
                     }

                     if(ga_type == 'default') {
                        successMsg = 'Thank you. We\'ll be in touch soon.';
                        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                        window.dataLayer.push({
                           'event': 'formSubmissionSuccess',
                           'type': type
                        });
                     } else if(ga_type == 'contactForm') {
                        successMsg = 'Thank you. We\'ll be in touch soon.';
                        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                        window.dataLayer.push({
                           'event': 'formSubmissionSuccess',
                           'type': type,
                           'helpType': helpType
                        });
                     } else if(ga_type == 'joinForm') {
                        successMsg = 'Thank you. We\'ll be in touch soon.';
                        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                        window.dataLayer.push({
                           'event': 'formSubmissionSuccess',
                           'type': type,
                           'propertyNum': propertyNum
                        });
                     }

                  } else if (data.success == 0) {
                     $.each(data.errors, function (key, value) {
                        errorMsg += '<p class="validate__error">' + value + '</p>';
                     });
                  }
               } else {
                  errorMsg = '<p class="validate__error">Something went wrong. Try again later.</p>';
               }

               // show success or error messages
               if (errorMsg != '') {
                  // promise for showing error msg then scrolling to it
                  var errorMsgPromise = new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
                     $(formId + ' #input-error').html(errorMsg).show(); // first show the error
                     resolve();
                  });

                  errorMsgPromise.then(function () {
                     if ($($(formId + ' #input-error p')[0]).isVisible('top')) { // then check if the errors are visible
                        $('html, body').animate({
                           scrollTop: $(formId + ' #input-error').offset().top - 90 // if they're not then scroll up to them
                        }, 700);
                     }
                  });
               } else if (successMsg != '') {
                  if ($(event.target).closest('form').hasClass('software-success')) {
                     window.location.href = "/software-success";
                     return;
                  } else if ($(event.target).closest('form').hasClass('join-success')) {
                     window.location.href = "/join/success";
                     return;
                  } else if ($(event.target).closest('form').hasClass('contact-success-us')) {
                     if (helpType == 'opt-join') {
                        window.location.href = "/contact/success";
                        return;
                     }
                  } else if ($(event.target).closest('form').hasClass('contact-success-uk')) {
                     window.location.href = "/contact/success";
                     return;
                  }
                  $(formId + ' #input-success').html(successMsg).show();
                  // clear form values
                  $(formId).each(function () {
                     this.reset();
                  });
               }
               // enable submit button
               $(formId + ' .input-submit').removeClass('disabled');
            });
      });
   });

   $('#ppc-input-submit').click(function (event) {

   if ($("#sticky-scroll").length) {
      $(window).scroll(function () {
         // if #sticky-scroll-target is below the top of the screen and it has class 'sticky-top': fadeOut #sticky-scroll, remove that class and unhide (side-effect of fade)
         if ($("#sticky-scroll-target").isVisible('top') != true && $("#sticky-scroll").hasClass('sticky-top')) {
            $("#sticky-scroll").fadeOut(100);
            setTimeout(function () {
               $("#sticky-scroll").removeClass('sticky-top');
               $("#sticky-scroll").show();
            }, 101);
         }
         // else if #sticky-scroll-target is above the top of the screen and it does not have class 'sticky-top': 'hide' #sticky-scroll, add that class and fadeIn
         else if ($("#sticky-scroll-target").isVisible('top') == true && !$("#sticky-scroll").hasClass('sticky-top')) {
            $("#sticky-scroll").hide();
            $("#sticky-scroll").addClass('sticky-top');
            $("#sticky-scroll").fadeIn(200);
         }
      })

   function getCookie(cname) {
      var name = cname + "=";
      var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
      var ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
      for(var i = 0; i <ca.length; i++) {
         var c = ca[i];
         while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
            c = c.substring(1);
         }
         if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
            return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
         }
      }
      return "";
   }

   /* Replace number for referrals via Google PPC ads */
   function checkGCLIDCode() {
      "use strict";

      var url = window.location.href;
      var gclid = null;
      var telNumber = "0203 399 8591";
      var isGCLID = 'isgclid';
      var telElement = jQuery('.contactTel');

      url = url.toLowerCase();
      gclid = localStorage.getItem(isGCLID);

      if (url.indexOf('gclid') != -1 || url.indexOf('gcid') != -1) {
         localStorage.setItem(isGCLID, 'true')
         gclid = 'true';
      }

      if (gclid && telNumber.length) {
         telElement.find('a').attr('href', 'tel:'+telNumber.replace(/ /g, ''));
         telElement.find('a').text(telNumber);
      }
      telElement.show();
   }
   checkGCLIDCode();
});
